# Skills · F · part 24

886 entries, feature-engineering-pipeline to feature-implementation. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **feature-engineering-pipeline**: Use this skill when designing, building, or refining machine learning feature engineering pipelines. Constructs reproducible, leakage-proof transformations: cyclical datetime sin/cos encoding, empirical Bayes smoothed target encoding, interaction term generation, frequency and ordinal encoding, and missing indicator columns. All transformers packaged inside scikit-learn Pipelines to guarantee strict train-only fitting, with a zero-dependency companion script for validation. ([abhay-2108/agent-foundry](https://github.com/abhay-2108/agent-foundry/tree/HEAD/skills/data-analysis/feature-engineering-pipeline))
- **feature-engineering-pipeline**: Feature engineering pipeline with Python transformations, Prolog consistency checks, and Hy fuzzy feature scoring. ([SpectreDeath/Em-Cubed](https://github.com/SpectreDeath/Em-Cubed/tree/HEAD/skills/FEATURE_ENGINEERING/feature-engineering-pipeline))
- **feature-engineering-pipeline**: Production-grade skill package for Feature Engineering Pipeline in data-mlops. ([usmedishetty/Antigravity-ECC](https://github.com/usmedishetty/Antigravity-ECC/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/feature-engineering-pipeline) · 2 copies)
- **feature-engineering-review**: The feature-engineering juror's checklist and grep tells for temporal correctness, leakage, training-serving skew, backfill anchoring, and feature versioning. ([KranzL/jje](https://github.com/KranzL/jje/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/feature-engineering-review) · 2 copies)
- **feature-engineering-saturation-detection**: Use when you have run 3+ feature engineering experiments with no improvement, or when distance to theoretical upper bound is &lt;15pp. Triggers when you suspect "I should stop optimizing features and try something else" — the most common failure mode is wasting weeks past the saturation point. ([topprismdata/cultivating-ml-agent](https://github.com/topprismdata/cultivating-ml-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/examples/feature-engineering-saturation-detection) · ★5)
- **feature-engineering-skill**: DATA_SIZE = 5 # Training data volume # 1-3: &lt;5K rows — features are the only lever, complexity won't help # 4-7: 5K–500K rows — features + moderate complexity # 8-10: &gt;500K rows — model complexity can compensate for missing features ([ayush488-glitch/ai-engineering-os](https://github.com/ayush488-glitch/ai-engineering-os/tree/HEAD/f1-the-prediction-engine/feature-engineering-skill) · ★19)
- **feature-engineering-specialist**: Design, validate, transform, and serve machine-learning features with point-in-time correctness, reproducibility, privacy, and online-offline consistency. ([ometere123/the-skills-bible](https://github.com/ometere123/the-skills-bible/tree/HEAD/skills/feature-engineering-specialist))
- **feature-engineering-systematically**: Use when adding, transforming, or selecting features for a model - requires features to be defined as pure, testable functions with explicit contracts ([creyesp/datapowers](https://github.com/creyesp/datapowers/tree/HEAD/skills/feature-engineering-systematically))
- **feature-engineering-tabular**: Engineer features for tabular models (aggregations, encodings, interactions) that add signal without leaking. Use when building features for a tabular ML or Kaggle problem, where features often matter more than the model. ([Amey-Thakur/AI-SKILLS](https://github.com/Amey-Thakur/AI-SKILLS/tree/HEAD/skills/data-science/feature-engineering-tabular) · ★14)
- **feature-engineering-toolkit**: Build numeric features (polynomial, log, sqrt, binning), categorical features (one-hot, ordinal, target/mean encoding with CV, frequency), interaction features, datetime features, and lag/rolling window features with closed='right' to prevent leakage. Use this skill when constructing features for tabular models. ([HossamBalaha/SkillStack](https://github.com/HossamBalaha/SkillStack/tree/HEAD/data-engineering/feature-engineering-toolkit))
- **feature-engineering-without-leakage**: Use when designing or auditing features for a model that predicts a future price move, to guarantee each feature is computable strictly before the target is realised; catches same-bar returns and label-derived inputs. ([HimanshuJ16/Algo-Trading-Skills](https://github.com/HimanshuJ16/Algo-Trading-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/feature-engineering-without-leakage))
- **feature-enhance**: Comprehensive feature-enhancement discovery skill. Points at any codebase — the current Claude Code workspace, a local source folder, or a Forgejo/Git repo URL — scans the full stack, inventories existing features, surfaces incomplete/stub work, then researches comparable apps and platforms to generate a prioritized list of value-add enhancements. Outputs a structured FEATURE_ENHANCEMENTS.md report. Trigger whenever the user says: "feature enhancement", "what features are we missing", "analyze my codebase for improvements", "compare to competitors", "what should I add next", "audit my app", "… ([frobinson47/solo-dev-suite](https://github.com/frobinson47/solo-dev-suite/tree/HEAD/skills/feature-enhance) · ★4)
- **feature-enhancement**: Lightweight enhancement workflow for an existing feature. Use when an existing feature needs additions or modifications based on user feedback — the need is basically known, so this skips the full discovery interview and goes straight to alignment, UX, and technical consultation. Use when the user says "I want to add X to the existing feature", "users asked for this change", "quick enhancement to the [feature]", or invokes /feature-enhancement. ([brennanmeadowcroft/development-plugins](https://github.com/brennanmeadowcroft/development-plugins/tree/HEAD/product-discovery/skills/feature-enhancement))

- **feature-evolution**: 功能迭代变更管理。对已完成开发闭环的功能进行增量修改、扩展或优化，生成变更影响分析和增量任务计划（适配 TDD 流程）。 ([MingYuePop/SpecForge](https://github.com/MingYuePop/SpecForge/tree/HEAD/V2/skills/feature-evolution) · ★275 · 2 copies)
- **feature-evolution**: 功能变更管理。当用户对一个已有规划（或已开发完成）的功能提出修改、补充或扩展需求时触发。覆盖两种场景：1）功能已开发完成，事后想迭代；2）功能开发进行中，中途想加东西或调整方向。触发词如'这个功能我想加一个XX'、'这里的逻辑需要调整'、'开发到一半发现还需要XX'、'想给XX功能扩展一下'。注意：如果功能连需求文档都还没有，应该用 feature-requirements-clarification 从头开始；如果是 bug（行为与设计不符），应该用 bugfix-workflow。 ([MingYuePop/SpecForge](https://github.com/MingYuePop/SpecForge/tree/HEAD/V3/skills/feature/feature-evolution) · ★275)
- **feature-evolution**: 功能迭代技能。当用户提到"功能迭代""需求变更""加功能""改需求""功能修改""功能调整""需求调整""改一下""功能变更""修改功能""调整需求"等触发词时激活。 以产品架构师视角，处理已完成功能的增量修改，先读取所有文档搞清现状， 分析变更类型（微调/扩展/重构），增量更新文档，只生成变更相关新任务。 硬规则：只做分析和规划，绝对不写代码。 ([w020316/cautious-broccoli](https://github.com/w020316/cautious-broccoli/tree/HEAD/workflow/feature-evolution))
- **feature-evolution-evaluation**: 特征进化确定性评估核心(G1~G6关卡/前置诊断/原料矩阵/多seed快筛/全量终判/台账/反馈/导出)。不含任何LLM环节。当用户说"评估这些候选特征""这个特征有没有用""结算这一轮""导出接受的特征"时使用;正常流程由feature-evolution-orchestration调用。 ([QFIN-tech/model-evo](https://github.com/QFIN-tech/model-evo/tree/HEAD/feature-mining-skills/feature-evolution-evaluation) · ★56)
- **feature-evolution-orchestration**: 特征进化编排--围绕当前建模任务持续挖掘有价值的特征。可挖性前置诊断(开跑前判ROI,避免在榨干的池里空转)+ 数据驱动候选生成(原料相关性矩阵黑名单,champion残差反推)+ 多seed降噪Stage A + 严苛全量Stage B。LLM提假设、确定性系统判G1-G6,只认valid/OOT稳定增益。当用户说"特征进化""特征挖掘""帮我找特征""提升模型效果的特征""feature evolution"时使用。 ([QFIN-tech/model-evo](https://github.com/QFIN-tech/model-evo/tree/HEAD/feature-mining-skills/feature-evolution-orchestration) · ★56)
